DengeERP V1.20.1 TÜM KAYNAK KODLARI — İnceleme Kopyası
Bu tek TXT, uygulamanın DERLEME GİRDİSİ değildir. Derleme için DengeERP.Api/ klasörünü kullanın.
Dosyalar orijinal klasör yapısıyla ayrıca ZIP içinde yer alır.
====================================================================================================

=================================== DOSYA: DengeERP.Api/Database/DengeERP_Update.sql ==========

USE DengeERP;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.WorkOrders','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.WorkOrders
    (
        WorkOrderId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
        WorkOrderNo NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
        UnitCode NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        WorkType NVARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
        CustomerName NVARCHAR(250) NOT NULL,
        ProjectName NVARCHAR(250) NULL,
        Location NVARCHAR(250) NULL,
        Subject NVARCHAR(250) NOT NULL,
        Description NVARCHAR(MAX) NULL,
        Priority NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT N'Normal',
        Status NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT N'Müşteri Talebi',
        CreatedByUserId INT NULL,
        CreatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
        RowVersion ROWVERSION
    );
END
GO

IF COL_LENGTH('dbo.WorkOrders','SystemName') IS NULL ALTER TABLE dbo.WorkOrders ADD SystemName NVARCHAR(150) NULL;
IF COL_LENGTH('dbo.WorkOrders','SerialNo') IS NULL ALTER TABLE dbo.WorkOrders ADD SerialNo NVARCHAR(200) NULL;
IF COL_LENGTH('dbo.WorkOrders','CustomerEmail') IS NULL ALTER TABLE dbo.WorkOrders ADD CustomerEmail NVARCHAR(250) NULL;
IF COL_LENGTH('dbo.WorkOrders','UpdatedAt') IS NULL ALTER TABLE dbo.WorkOrders ADD UpdatedAt DATETIME2 NULL;
IF COL_LENGTH('dbo.WorkOrders','ClosedAt') IS NULL ALTER TABLE dbo.WorkOrders ADD ClosedAt DATETIME2 NULL;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.WorkOrderAssignments','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.WorkOrderAssignments
    (
        AssignmentId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
        WorkOrderId BIGINT NOT NULL,
        AssignedName NVARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
        AssignmentRole NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT N'Teknisyen',
        IsActive BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        AssignedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
        CONSTRAINT FK_WorkOrderAssignments_WorkOrders FOREIGN KEY (WorkOrderId) REFERENCES dbo.WorkOrders(WorkOrderId)
    );
END
GO

IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.WorkOrderPlans','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.WorkOrderPlans
    (
        WorkOrderPlanId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
        WorkOrderId BIGINT NOT NULL,
        PlanDate DATE NOT NULL,
        StartTime TIME NULL,
        EstimatedMinutes INT NULL,
        VehiclePlate NVARCHAR(30) NULL,
        RouteGroupNo NVARCHAR(50) NULL,
        PlanNote NVARCHAR(1000) NULL,
        IsActive BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        PlannedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
        CONSTRAINT FK_WorkOrderPlans_WorkOrders FOREIGN KEY (WorkOrderId) REFERENCES dbo.WorkOrders(WorkOrderId)
    );
END
GO

IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.WorkOrderDevices','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.WorkOrderDevices
    (
        WorkOrderDeviceId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
        WorkOrderId BIGINT NOT NULL,
        SerialNo NVARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
        ProductName NVARCHAR(200) NULL,
        Brand NVARCHAR(120) NULL,
        Model NVARCHAR(120) NULL,
        IsActive BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        AddedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
        CONSTRAINT FK_WorkOrderDevices_WorkOrders FOREIGN KEY (WorkOrderId) REFERENCES dbo.WorkOrders(WorkOrderId)
    );
END
GO

IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.WorkOrderStatusLogs','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.WorkOrderStatusLogs
    (
        StatusLogId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
        WorkOrderId BIGINT NOT NULL,
        OldStatus NVARCHAR(100) NULL,
        NewStatus NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        Note NVARCHAR(1000) NULL,
        ChangedByUserId INT NULL,
        ChangedByName NVARCHAR(150) NULL,
        ChangedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
        CONSTRAINT FK_WorkOrderStatusLogs_WorkOrders FOREIGN KEY (WorkOrderId) REFERENCES dbo.WorkOrders(WorkOrderId)
    );
END
GO

IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.WorkTypeRules','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.WorkTypeRules
    (
        RuleId INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
        UnitCode NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        WorkType NVARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
        SerialRequiredOnCreate BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        SerialRequiredOnClose BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        IsActive BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        CONSTRAINT UQ_WorkTypeRules UNIQUE(UnitCode,WorkType)
    );
END
GO

MERGE dbo.WorkTypeRules AS t
USING (VALUES
(N'Güvenlik Sistemleri',N'Atölye Tamir',1,1),
(N'Güvenlik Sistemleri',N'Saha Arızası',0,1),
(N'Güvenlik Sistemleri',N'Saha Kurulumu / Devreye Alma',0,1),
(N'Güvenlik Sistemleri',N'Kontrol / Test',0,1),
(N'Haberleşme',N'Atölye Teknik Servis',1,1),
(N'Haberleşme',N'Saha Arızası',0,1),
(N'Haberleşme',N'Saha Kurulumu',0,1),
(N'Haberleşme',N'Kiralık El Telsizi Hazırlığı',1,1),
(N'Haberleşme',N'Kiradan Dönen El Telsizi Testi',1,1),
(N'Mobilite',N'Kurulum',0,1),
(N'Mobilite',N'Arıza',0,1),
(N'Araç Takip',N'Kurulum',0,1),
(N'Araç Takip',N'Arıza',0,1),
(N'Araç Takip',N'Bakım',0,1),
(N'POC',N'Uzaktan Müdahale',0,0),
(N'POC',N'Telefonla Destek',0,0),
(N'POC',N'Yerinde Müdahale',0,0)
) AS s(UnitCode,WorkType,SerialRequiredOnCreate,SerialRequiredOnClose)
ON t.UnitCode=s.UnitCode AND t.WorkType=s.WorkType
WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET SerialRequiredOnCreate=s.SerialRequiredOnCreate,SerialRequiredOnClose=s.SerialRequiredOnClose,IsActive=1
WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT(UnitCode,WorkType,SerialRequiredOnCreate,SerialRequiredOnClose) VALUES(s.UnitCode,s.WorkType,s.SerialRequiredOnCreate,s.SerialRequiredOnClose);
GO

IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.ErpUsers','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.ErpUsers
    (
        UserId INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
        Username NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
        PasswordHash NVARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
        PasswordSalt NVARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
        FullName NVARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
        RoleName NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        AllowedUnits NVARCHAR(MAX) NOT NULL DEFAULT N'',
        Permissions NVARCHAR(MAX) NOT NULL DEFAULT N'',
        IsActive BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        MustChangePassword BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        CreatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
        UpdatedAt DATETIME2 NULL
    );
END
GO

-- Pilot V1.1: Yeni ERP kullanıcıları ile eski AppUsers ilişkisini birbirinden ayır.
-- Mevcut CreatedByUserId kolonu eski sistemle uyumluluk için korunur ve nullable yapılır.
IF COL_LENGTH('dbo.WorkOrders','CreatedByUserId') IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
    ALTER TABLE dbo.WorkOrders ALTER COLUMN CreatedByUserId INT NULL;
END
GO

IF COL_LENGTH('dbo.WorkOrders','CreatedByErpUserId') IS NULL
BEGIN
    ALTER TABLE dbo.WorkOrders ADD CreatedByErpUserId INT NULL;
END
GO

IF NOT EXISTS
(
    SELECT 1
    FROM sys.foreign_keys
    WHERE name = 'FK_WorkOrders_ErpUsers'
      AND parent_object_id = OBJECT_ID('dbo.WorkOrders')
)
BEGIN
    ALTER TABLE dbo.WorkOrders
    ADD CONSTRAINT FK_WorkOrders_ErpUsers
        FOREIGN KEY (CreatedByErpUserId) REFERENCES dbo.ErpUsers(UserId);
END
GO

IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.Offers','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.Offers
    (
        OfferId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
        OfferNo NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
        WorkOrderId BIGINT NULL,
        UnitCode NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        CustomerName NVARCHAR(250) NOT NULL,
        CustomerEmail NVARCHAR(250) NULL,
        Status NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT N'Taslak',
        ValidUntil DATE NULL,
        Notes NVARCHAR(1000) NULL,
        SubTotal DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        DiscountTotal DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        VatTotal DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        GrandTotal DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        CreatedByUserId INT NULL,
        CreatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
        ApprovedByUserId INT NULL,
        ApprovedAt DATETIME2 NULL,
        SentAt DATETIME2 NULL,
        CONSTRAINT FK_Offers_WorkOrders FOREIGN KEY (WorkOrderId) REFERENCES dbo.WorkOrders(WorkOrderId)
    );
END
GO

IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.OfferItems','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.OfferItems
    (
        OfferItemId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
        OfferId BIGINT NOT NULL,
        ItemCode NVARCHAR(100) NULL,
        Description NVARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
        Quantity DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        UnitPrice DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        DiscountPercent DECIMAL(9,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        VatPercent DECIMAL(9,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 20,
        CONSTRAINT FK_OfferItems_Offers FOREIGN KEY (OfferId) REFERENCES dbo.Offers(OfferId) ON DELETE CASCADE
    );
END
GO

IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.EmailLogs','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.EmailLogs
    (
        EmailLogId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
        OfferId BIGINT NULL,
        Recipient NVARCHAR(250) NOT NULL,
        Subject NVARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
        Status NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
        ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(1000) NULL,
        SentByUserId INT NULL,
        SentAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
        CONSTRAINT FK_EmailLogs_Offers FOREIGN KEY (OfferId) REFERENCES dbo.Offers(OfferId)
    );
END
GO

IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE name='IX_WorkOrders_Unit_Status' AND object_id=OBJECT_ID('dbo.WorkOrders'))
    CREATE INDEX IX_WorkOrders_Unit_Status ON dbo.WorkOrders(UnitCode,Status,CreatedAt DESC);
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE name='IX_WorkOrderAssignments_Active' AND object_id=OBJECT_ID('dbo.WorkOrderAssignments'))
    CREATE INDEX IX_WorkOrderAssignments_Active ON dbo.WorkOrderAssignments(WorkOrderId,IsActive,AssignmentRole);
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE name='IX_WorkOrderPlans_Active' AND object_id=OBJECT_ID('dbo.WorkOrderPlans'))
    CREATE INDEX IX_WorkOrderPlans_Active ON dbo.WorkOrderPlans(WorkOrderId,IsActive);
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE name='IX_WorkOrderDevices_Active' AND object_id=OBJECT_ID('dbo.WorkOrderDevices'))
    CREATE INDEX IX_WorkOrderDevices_Active ON dbo.WorkOrderDevices(WorkOrderId,IsActive);
GO

-- Pilot V1.4: Kiralama + Personel / Organizasyon
IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.RentalProducts','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.RentalProducts
    (
        RentalProductId INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
        UnitCode NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        Category NVARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
        ProductCode NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        ProductName NVARCHAR(250) NOT NULL,
        RequiresSerial BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        IsActive BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        DisplayOrder INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        CONSTRAINT UQ_RentalProducts UNIQUE(UnitCode,ProductCode)
    );
END
GO

USING (VALUES

(N'Güvenlik Sistemleri',N'Akordiyon Bariyer',N'BS-2000',N'BS-2000 Akordiyon Bariyer',0,130),

(N'Güvenlik Sistemleri',N'Güvenlik Bariyeri',N'ST-3000',N'ST-3000 Çift Katlı NATO Güvenlik Bariyeri',0,190),
(N'Güvenlik Sistemleri',N'Güvenlik Bariyeri',N'ST-4000',N'ST-4000 Çit Bariyer',0,200),
(N'Güvenlik Sistemleri',N'Güvenlik Bariyeri',N'ST-2000',N'ST-2000 Tek Katlı NATO Bariyer',0,210),
(N'Güvenlik Sistemleri',N'Güvenlik Bariyeri',N'ST-5000',N'ST-5000 Bisiklet Bariyeri',0,220),
(N'Haberleşme',N'Telsiz',N'HYT-DMR',N'Hytera Dijital / DMR Telsiz',1,300),
(N'Haberleşme',N'Telsiz',N'HYT-TETRA',N'Hytera TETRA Telsiz',1,310),
(N'Haberleşme',N'Telsiz',N'HYT-LTE-POC',N'Hytera LTE / PoC Telsiz',1,320)
) AS s(UnitCode,Category,ProductCode,ProductName,RequiresSerial,DisplayOrder)
ON t.UnitCode=s.UnitCode AND t.ProductCode=s.ProductCode
WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET Category=s.Category,ProductName=s.ProductName,RequiresSerial=s.RequiresSerial,IsActive=1,DisplayOrder=s.DisplayOrder
WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT(UnitCode,Category,ProductCode,ProductName,RequiresSerial,IsActive,DisplayOrder)
VALUES(s.UnitCode,s.Category,s.ProductCode,s.ProductName,s.RequiresSerial,1,s.DisplayOrder);
GO

IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.RentalOrders','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.RentalOrders
    (
        RentalOrderId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
        RentalNo NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
        UnitCode NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        Category NVARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
        RentalProductId INT NULL,
        ProductCode NVARCHAR(100) NULL,
        ProductName NVARCHAR(250) NOT NULL,
        CustomerName NVARCHAR(250) NOT NULL,
        CustomerEmail NVARCHAR(250) NULL,
        ProjectName NVARCHAR(250) NULL,
        Location NVARCHAR(250) NULL,
        ContactName NVARCHAR(150) NULL,
        ContactPhone NVARCHAR(80) NULL,
        Quantity INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        SerialNumbers NVARCHAR(MAX) NULL,
        StartDate DATE NOT NULL,
        EndDate DATE NOT NULL,
        Status NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT N'Talep',
        ResponsibleName NVARCHAR(150) NULL,
        Notes NVARCHAR(1500) NULL,
        CreatedByUserId INT NULL,
        CreatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
        UpdatedAt DATETIME2 NULL,
        ClosedAt DATETIME2 NULL,
        CONSTRAINT FK_RentalOrders_RentalProducts FOREIGN KEY (RentalProductId) REFERENCES dbo.RentalProducts(RentalProductId),
        CONSTRAINT FK_RentalOrders_ErpUsers FOREIGN KEY (CreatedByUserId) REFERENCES dbo.ErpUsers(UserId)
    );
END
GO

IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE name='IX_RentalOrders_Unit_Status' AND object_id=OBJECT_ID('dbo.RentalOrders'))
    CREATE INDEX IX_RentalOrders_Unit_Status ON dbo.RentalOrders(UnitCode,Status,StartDate DESC);
GO

IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.Personnel','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.Personnel
    (
        PersonnelId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
        FullName NVARCHAR(180) NOT NULL,
        DepartmentName NVARCHAR(180) NOT NULL,
        AdditionalDepartments NVARCHAR(MAX) NULL,
        Title NVARCHAR(180) NULL,
        UnitCode NVARCHAR(100) NULL,
        ManagerName NVARCHAR(180) NULL,
        StartDate DATE NULL,
        EndDate DATE NULL,
        IsActive BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        ErpUserId INT NULL,
        Notes NVARCHAR(1000) NULL,
        CreatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
        UpdatedAt DATETIME2 NULL,
        CONSTRAINT FK_Personnel_ErpUsers FOREIGN KEY (ErpUserId) REFERENCES dbo.ErpUsers(UserId)
    );
END
GO

(N'Ahmet Erzurum',N'GMY',NULL,N'Genel Müdür Yardımcısı',NULL,N'Nurettin Erzurum'),

INSERT INTO dbo.Personnel(FullName,DepartmentName,AdditionalDepartments,Title,UnitCode,ManagerName,IsActive)
SELECT o.FullName,o.DepartmentName,o.AdditionalDepartments,o.Title,o.UnitCode,o.ManagerName,1
FROM @Org o
WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.Personnel p WHERE p.FullName=o.FullName);
GO

UPDATE p
SET ErpUserId=u.UserId, UpdatedAt=SYSDATETIME()
FROM dbo.Personnel p
JOIN dbo.ErpUsers u ON u.FullName=p.FullName
WHERE p.ErpUserId IS NULL;
GO



=================================== DOSYA: DengeERP.Api/Database/DengeERP_V15_Schema.sql ==========


-- Pilot V1.5: Güvenlik cihaz kütüğü + bakım sözleşmeleri + Haberleşme atölye CRM alanları
IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.SecurityDevices','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.SecurityDevices
    (
        SecurityDeviceId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
        SourceKey NVARCHAR(80) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
        SourceRowNo INT NULL,
        DeviceType NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        Brand NVARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
        Model NVARCHAR(250) NOT NULL,
        ProductRaw NVARCHAR(500) NULL,
        SerialNo NVARCHAR(200) NULL,
        CustomerName NVARCHAR(250) NULL,
        Location NVARCHAR(250) NULL,
        ContactName NVARCHAR(180) NULL,
        DeliveryDateText NVARCHAR(100) NULL,
        InstallStatus NVARCHAR(100) NULL,
        Notes NVARCHAR(1000) NULL,
        IsActive BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        ImportedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME()
    );
END
GO

IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE name='IX_SecurityDevices_Search' AND object_id=OBJECT_ID('dbo.SecurityDevices'))
    CREATE INDEX IX_SecurityDevices_Search ON dbo.SecurityDevices(DeviceType,Brand,Model,SerialNo);
GO

IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.MaintenanceContracts','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.MaintenanceContracts
    (
        MaintenanceContractId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
        SourceKey NVARCHAR(80) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
        CustomerName NVARCHAR(250) NOT NULL,
        CurrentAddress NVARCHAR(500) NULL,
        ProductsText NVARCHAR(500) NULL,
        FrequencyText NVARCHAR(100) NULL,
        AnnualFrequency INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        MaintenanceFeeText NVARCHAR(100) NULL,
        PeriodText NVARCHAR(150) NULL,
        CompletedCount INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        ContractTotal DECIMAL(18,2) NULL,
        Total2026 DECIMAL(18,2) NULL,
        IsActive BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        ImportedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
        UpdatedAt DATETIME2 NULL
    );
END
GO

IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.MaintenancePlans','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.MaintenancePlans
    (
        MaintenancePlanId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
        MaintenanceContractId BIGINT NOT NULL,
        PlanYear INT NOT NULL,
        SequenceNo INT NOT NULL,
        TargetDate DATE NOT NULL,
        IsCompleted BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        CompletedAt DATETIME2 NULL,
        GeneratedWorkOrderId BIGINT NULL,
        CreatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
        CONSTRAINT FK_MaintenancePlans_Contracts FOREIGN KEY (MaintenanceContractId) REFERENCES dbo.MaintenanceContracts(MaintenanceContractId),
        CONSTRAINT FK_MaintenancePlans_WorkOrders FOREIGN KEY (GeneratedWorkOrderId) REFERENCES dbo.WorkOrders(WorkOrderId),
        CONSTRAINT UQ_MaintenancePlans UNIQUE(MaintenanceContractId,PlanYear,SequenceNo)
    );
END
GO

IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE name='IX_MaintenancePlans_Due' AND object_id=OBJECT_ID('dbo.MaintenancePlans'))
    CREATE INDEX IX_MaintenancePlans_Due ON dbo.MaintenancePlans(TargetDate,IsCompleted,GeneratedWorkOrderId);
GO

IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.WorkshopTickets','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.WorkshopTickets
    (
        WorkshopTicketId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
        WorkshopNo NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
        WorkOrderId BIGINT NULL,
        LogoFormNo NVARCHAR(80) NULL,
        LogoMainServiceNo NVARCHAR(80) NULL,
        CompanyName NVARCHAR(250) NOT NULL,
        CompanyAddress NVARCHAR(500) NULL,
        ContactName NVARCHAR(180) NULL,
        CustomerEmail NVARCHAR(250) NULL,
        CustomerPhone NVARCHAR(100) NULL,
        EntryDate DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
        EstimatedFinishDate DATE NULL,
        CompletionDate DATETIME2 NULL,
        IntegrationCompany NVARCHAR(150) NULL,
        ServiceType NVARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
        Status NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT N'Yeni Kayıt',
        RepairAmount DECIMAL(18,2) NULL,
        ReceiveType NVARCHAR(120) NULL,
        ReceivedBy NVARCHAR(180) NULL,
        DeliveredBy NVARCHAR(180) NULL,
        DeliveryType NVARCHAR(120) NULL,
        CargoTrackingNo NVARCHAR(150) NULL,
        AssignedTo NVARCHAR(180) NULL,
        Brand NVARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
        Model NVARCHAR(180) NULL,
        SerialNo NVARCHAR(180) NOT NULL,
        WarrantyStartDate DATE NULL,
        WarrantyEndDate DATE NULL,
        WarrantyStatus NVARCHAR(100) NULL,
        Accessory1 NVARCHAR(120) NULL,
        Accessory2 NVARCHAR(120) NULL,
        Accessory3 NVARCHAR(120) NULL,
        Accessory4 NVARCHAR(120) NULL,
        Accessory5 NVARCHAR(120) NULL,
        ProjectName NVARCHAR(250) NULL,
        Notes NVARCHAR(MAX) NULL,
        CustomerComplaint NVARCHAR(MAX) NULL,
        DeviceCondition NVARCHAR(MAX) NULL,
        RepairInfo NVARCHAR(MAX) NULL,
        CreatedByUserId INT NULL,
        CreatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
        UpdatedAt DATETIME2 NULL,
        CONSTRAINT FK_WorkshopTickets_WorkOrders FOREIGN KEY (WorkOrderId) REFERENCES dbo.WorkOrders(WorkOrderId),
        CONSTRAINT FK_WorkshopTickets_ErpUsers FOREIGN KEY (CreatedByUserId) REFERENCES dbo.ErpUsers(UserId)
    );
END
GO

IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE name='IX_WorkshopTickets_Status' AND object_id=OBJECT_ID('dbo.WorkshopTickets'))
    CREATE INDEX IX_WorkshopTickets_Status ON dbo.WorkshopTickets(Status,EntryDate DESC);
GO



MERGE dbo.WorkTypeRules AS t
USING (VALUES
(N'Güvenlik Sistemleri',N'Planlı Bakım',0,1)
) AS s(UnitCode,WorkType,SerialRequiredOnCreate,SerialRequiredOnClose)
ON t.UnitCode=s.UnitCode AND t.WorkType=s.WorkType
WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET SerialRequiredOnCreate=s.SerialRequiredOnCreate,SerialRequiredOnClose=s.SerialRequiredOnClose,IsActive=1
WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT(UnitCode,WorkType,SerialRequiredOnCreate,SerialRequiredOnClose)
VALUES(s.UnitCode,s.WorkType,s.SerialRequiredOnCreate,s.SerialRequiredOnClose);
GO

-- Mevcut pilot kullanıcılarına yeni V1.5 modül yetkilerini rol bazında ekle.
UPDATE dbo.ErpUsers
SET Permissions = Permissions + CASE WHEN Permissions=N'' THEN N'' ELSE N'|' END + N'devices.view'
WHERE RoleName IN (N'Üst Yönetim',N'Teknik Servis Müdürü',N'Teknik Servis Müdür Yardımcısı',N'Genel Koordinatör',N'Operasyon Uzmanı',N'Şef',N'Teknisyen')
  AND N'|'+Permissions+N'|' NOT LIKE N'%|devices.view|%';

UPDATE dbo.ErpUsers
SET Permissions = Permissions + CASE WHEN Permissions=N'' THEN N'' ELSE N'|' END + N'maintenance.view'
WHERE RoleName IN (N'Üst Yönetim',N'Teknik Servis Müdürü',N'Teknik Servis Müdür Yardımcısı',N'Genel Koordinatör',N'Operasyon Uzmanı',N'Şef')
  AND N'|'+Permissions+N'|' NOT LIKE N'%|maintenance.view|%';

UPDATE dbo.ErpUsers
SET Permissions = Permissions + CASE WHEN Permissions=N'' THEN N'' ELSE N'|' END + N'maintenance.manage'
WHERE RoleName IN (N'Üst Yönetim',N'Teknik Servis Müdürü',N'Teknik Servis Müdür Yardımcısı',N'Şef')
  AND N'|'+Permissions+N'|' NOT LIKE N'%|maintenance.manage|%';

UPDATE dbo.ErpUsers
SET Permissions = Permissions + CASE WHEN Permissions=N'' THEN N'' ELSE N'|' END + N'workshop.view'
WHERE RoleName IN (N'Üst Yönetim',N'Teknik Servis Müdürü',N'Teknik Servis Müdür Yardımcısı',N'Genel Koordinatör',N'Operasyon Uzmanı',N'Şef',N'Teknisyen')
  AND N'|'+Permissions+N'|' NOT LIKE N'%|workshop.view|%';

UPDATE dbo.ErpUsers
SET Permissions = Permissions + CASE WHEN Permissions=N'' THEN N'' ELSE N'|' END + N'workshop.manage'
WHERE RoleName IN (N'Üst Yönetim',N'Teknik Servis Müdürü',N'Teknik Servis Müdür Yardımcısı',N'Şef',N'Teknisyen')
  AND N'|'+Permissions+N'|' NOT LIKE N'%|workshop.manage|%';
GO

    SourceRowNo=s.SourceRowNo,DeviceType=s.DeviceType,Brand=s.Brand,Model=s.Model,ProductRaw=s.ProductRaw,
    SerialNo=s.SerialNo,CustomerName=s.CustomerName,Location=s.Location,ContactName=s.ContactName,
    DeliveryDateText=s.DeliveryDateText,InstallStatus=s.InstallStatus,Notes=s.Notes,IsActive=1
WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT(SourceKey,SourceRowNo,DeviceType,Brand,Model,ProductRaw,SerialNo,CustomerName,Location,ContactName,DeliveryDateText,InstallStatus,Notes,IsActive)
VALUES(s.SourceKey,s.SourceRowNo,s.DeviceType,s.Brand,s.Model,s.ProductRaw,s.SerialNo,s.CustomerName,s.Location,s.ContactName,s.DeliveryDateText,s.InstallStatus,s.Notes,1);
GO

    SerialNo=s.SerialNo,CustomerName=s.CustomerName,Location=s.Location,ContactName=s.ContactName,

(N'BAKIM-0085',N'ZİRAAT BANKASI',N'6 AYDA BİR',N'5 SENE BOYUNCA',N'İLK BAKIM',0,N'ARALIK 2026''DA',NULL,0,NULL,NULL),
(N'BAKIM-0086',N'SANKO HOLDİNG',N'SANKO HOLDİNG',N'1 KAPI DEDEKTÖRÜ',NULL,0,N'7000 TL + KDV',NULL,0,NULL,7000.0),
(N'BAKIM-0087',N'PAM MÜLK YÖNETİMİ',N'PAM MÜLK YÖNETİMİ',N'6 X-RAY 9 KAPI DEDEKTÖRÜ',N'YILDA 2 ADET',2,N'49600 TL + KDV',N'01.09.2026-01.09.2027',0,NULL,49600.0),
(N'BAKIM-0088',N'KENTPARK AVM',N'KENTPARK AVM',N'11 X-RAY 8 KAPI DEDEKTÖÜ',N'YILDA 1 ADET',1,N'35000 TL + KDV',N'01.09.2026-01.09.2027',1,NULL,35000.0)
) AS s(SourceKey,CustomerName,CurrentAddress,ProductsText,FrequencyText,AnnualFrequency,MaintenanceFeeText,PeriodText,CompletedCount,ContractTotal,Total2026)
ON t.SourceKey=s.SourceKey
WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET
    CustomerName=s.CustomerName,CurrentAddress=s.CurrentAddress,ProductsText=s.ProductsText,FrequencyText=s.FrequencyText,
    AnnualFrequency=s.AnnualFrequency,MaintenanceFeeText=s.MaintenanceFeeText,PeriodText=s.PeriodText,
    CompletedCount=CASE WHEN t.CompletedCount>s.CompletedCount THEN t.CompletedCount ELSE s.CompletedCount END,
    ContractTotal=s.ContractTotal,Total2026=s.Total2026,IsActive=1,UpdatedAt=SYSDATETIME()
WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT(SourceKey,CustomerName,CurrentAddress,ProductsText,FrequencyText,AnnualFrequency,MaintenanceFeeText,PeriodText,CompletedCount,ContractTotal,Total2026,IsActive)
VALUES(s.SourceKey,s.CustomerName,s.CurrentAddress,s.ProductsText,s.FrequencyText,s.AnnualFrequency,s.MaintenanceFeeText,s.PeriodText,s.CompletedCount,s.ContractTotal,s.Total2026,1);
GO



=================================== DOSYA: DengeERP.Api/Database/DengeERP_V17_Schema.sql ==========

-- V1.7: Yalnızca yeni tablo; eski kayıtlar korunur.
IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.MaintenanceDeviceItems','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
 CREATE TABLE dbo.MaintenanceDeviceItems(
  MaintenanceDeviceItemId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
  MaintenancePlanId BIGINT NOT NULL,
  SecurityDeviceId BIGINT NULL,
  DeviceType NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  DeviceOrdinal INT NOT NULL,
  SerialNo NVARCHAR(200) NULL,
  Status NVARCHAR(30) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_MDI_Status DEFAULT N'Bekliyor',
  CompletedBy NVARCHAR(180) NULL,
  CompletedAt DATETIME2 NULL,
  Notes NVARCHAR(1000) NULL,
  CreatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_MDI_Created DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
  CONSTRAINT FK_MDI_Plan FOREIGN KEY(MaintenancePlanId) REFERENCES dbo.MaintenancePlans(MaintenancePlanId),
  CONSTRAINT FK_MDI_Device FOREIGN KEY(SecurityDeviceId) REFERENCES dbo.SecurityDevices(SecurityDeviceId),
  CONSTRAINT UQ_MDI_PlanTypeOrdinal UNIQUE(MaintenancePlanId,DeviceType,DeviceOrdinal)
 );
END
GO



=================================== DOSYA: DengeERP.Api/Database/DengeERP_V18_DeviceWorkOrders.sql ==========

-- DengeERP V1.8: Her cihaz icin bagimsiz PB is emri ve dijital servis/bakim formu.
-- Yalnizca eksik kolon ve tablolar eklenir, eski satirlar silinmez.
IF COL_LENGTH('dbo.MaintenanceDeviceItems','WorkOrderId') IS NULL
    ALTER TABLE dbo.MaintenanceDeviceItems ADD WorkOrderId BIGINT NULL;
GO
IF COL_LENGTH('dbo.MaintenanceDeviceItems','Zone') IS NULL
    ALTER TABLE dbo.MaintenanceDeviceItems ADD Zone NVARCHAR(250) NULL;
GO
IF COL_LENGTH('dbo.MaintenanceDeviceItems','PerformedWork') IS NULL
    ALTER TABLE dbo.MaintenanceDeviceItems ADD PerformedWork NVARCHAR(MAX) NULL;
GO
IF COL_LENGTH('dbo.MaintenanceDeviceItems','SpareParts') IS NULL
    ALTER TABLE dbo.MaintenanceDeviceItems ADD SpareParts NVARCHAR(1000) NULL;
GO
IF COL_LENGTH('dbo.MaintenanceDeviceItems','ServiceResult') IS NULL
    ALTER TABLE dbo.MaintenanceDeviceItems ADD ServiceResult NVARCHAR(80) NULL;
GO
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.foreign_keys WHERE name = 'FK_MDI_IndividualWorkOrder')
BEGIN
    ALTER TABLE dbo.MaintenanceDeviceItems ADD CONSTRAINT FK_MDI_IndividualWorkOrder
    FOREIGN KEY(WorkOrderId) REFERENCES dbo.WorkOrders(WorkOrderId);
END
GO
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE name='UX_MDI_WorkOrderId' AND object_id=OBJECT_ID('dbo.MaintenanceDeviceItems'))
BEGIN
    CREATE UNIQUE NONCLUSTERED INDEX UX_MDI_WorkOrderId
    ON dbo.MaintenanceDeviceItems(WorkOrderId) WHERE WorkOrderId IS NOT NULL;
END
GO
IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.MaintenanceDeviceChecks','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.MaintenanceDeviceChecks
    (
        MaintenanceDeviceCheckId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
        MaintenanceDeviceItemId BIGINT NOT NULL,
        CheckCode NVARCHAR(60) NOT NULL,
        CheckLabel NVARCHAR(350) NOT NULL,
        CheckResult NVARCHAR(40) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_MDChecks_Result DEFAULT N'Bekliyor',
        CheckedBy NVARCHAR(180) NULL,
        CheckedAt DATETIME2 NULL,
        CONSTRAINT FK_MDChecks_DeviceItem FOREIGN KEY(MaintenanceDeviceItemId)
           REFERENCES dbo.MaintenanceDeviceItems(MaintenanceDeviceItemId),
        CONSTRAINT UQ_MDChecks_DeviceItem_Code UNIQUE(MaintenanceDeviceItemId,CheckCode)
    );
END
GO



=================================== DOSYA: DengeERP.Api/Database/ZZ_V110_WorkOrderTimeLogs.sql ==========

-- V1.10 - Gerçek kişi bazlı çalışma süresi. Tekrar çalıştırılabilir / mevcut kayıtlara dokunmaz.
IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.WorkOrderTimeLogs', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.WorkOrderTimeLogs
    (
        TimeLogId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
        WorkOrderId BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.WorkOrders(WorkOrderId),
        TechnicianName NVARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
        ActivityType NVARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
        StartedAt DATETIME2(0) NOT NULL,
        EndedAt DATETIME2(0) NOT NULL,
        LocationNote NVARCHAR(250) NULL,
        WorkNote NVARCHAR(1000) NULL,
        CreatedByUserId INT NULL,
        CreatedByName NVARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
        CreatedAt DATETIME2(0) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_WorkOrderTimeLogs_CreatedAt DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
        IsVoided BIT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_WorkOrderTimeLogs_IsVoided DEFAULT (0),
        VoidedAt DATETIME2(0) NULL,
        VoidedByName NVARCHAR(200) NULL,
        CONSTRAINT CK_WorkOrderTimeLogs_ValidTimes CHECK (EndedAt > StartedAt AND DATEDIFF(MINUTE,StartedAt,EndedAt) <= 1080),
        CONSTRAINT CK_WorkOrderTimeLogs_Activity CHECK (ActivityType IN (N'Saha Çalışması',N'Yol / Ulaşım',N'Bekleme',N'Uzaktan Destek',N'Atölye',N'Test / Kontrol',N'Diğer'))
    );
END;
GO
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE object_id=OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.WorkOrderTimeLogs') AND name=N'IX_WorkOrderTimeLogs_TechnicianStart')
    CREATE INDEX IX_WorkOrderTimeLogs_TechnicianStart ON dbo.WorkOrderTimeLogs(TechnicianName,StartedAt) INCLUDE (EndedAt,WorkOrderId,IsVoided);
GO
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE object_id=OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.WorkOrderTimeLogs') AND name=N'IX_WorkOrderTimeLogs_WorkOrder')
    CREATE INDEX IX_WorkOrderTimeLogs_WorkOrder ON dbo.WorkOrderTimeLogs(WorkOrderId) INCLUDE (IsVoided,StartedAt,EndedAt);
GO

=================================== DOSYA: DengeERP.Api/Database/ZZ_V112_RentalInventoryOperations.sql ==========

-- V1.12 - Kiralama çoklu ürün, çoklu irtibat, depolar, envanter ve süreç geçmişi.
-- Tekrar çalıştırılabilir; mevcut kiralamaları ve kullanıcı verilerini silmez.
IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.RentalOrderItems','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
  CREATE TABLE dbo.RentalOrderItems(
    RentalOrderItemId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
    RentalOrderId BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.RentalOrders(RentalOrderId),
    RentalProductId INT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.RentalProducts(RentalProductId),
    Quantity INT NOT NULL CHECK(Quantity>0),
    SerialNumbers NVARCHAR(MAX) NULL,
    Notes NVARCHAR(1000) NULL,
    CreatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME()
  );
END
GO
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE name='IX_RentalOrderItems_Rental' AND object_id=OBJECT_ID('dbo.RentalOrderItems'))
  CREATE INDEX IX_RentalOrderItems_Rental ON dbo.RentalOrderItems(RentalOrderId);
GO
IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.RentalOrderContacts','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
  CREATE TABLE dbo.RentalOrderContacts(
    RentalOrderContactId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
    RentalOrderId BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.RentalOrders(RentalOrderId),
    ContactType NVARCHAR(60) NOT NULL DEFAULT N'Saha Operasyon',
    FullName NVARCHAR(180) NOT NULL,
    JobTitle NVARCHAR(180) NULL,
    Phone NVARCHAR(80) NULL,
    Email NVARCHAR(250) NULL,
    Notes NVARCHAR(500) NULL
  );
END
GO
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE name='IX_RentalOrderContacts_Rental' AND object_id=OBJECT_ID('dbo.RentalOrderContacts'))
  CREATE INDEX IX_RentalOrderContacts_Rental ON dbo.RentalOrderContacts(RentalOrderId);
GO
IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.RentalWarehouses','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
 CREATE TABLE dbo.RentalWarehouses(
  RentalWarehouseId INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
  Code NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  Name NVARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
  IsActive BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1
 );
END
GO
MERGE dbo.RentalWarehouses AS target
USING (VALUES (N'DEPO-1',N'Depo 1'),(N'DEPO-2',N'Depo 2'),(N'DEPO-3',N'Depo 3')) AS source(Code,Name)
ON target.Code=source.Code
WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT(Code,Name) VALUES(source.Code,source.Name);
GO
IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.RentalInventory','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
 CREATE TABLE dbo.RentalInventory(
  RentalInventoryId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
  RentalProductId INT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.RentalProducts(RentalProductId),
  RentalWarehouseId INT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.RentalWarehouses(RentalWarehouseId),
  SerialNo NVARCHAR(200) NULL,
  Quantity INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK(Quantity>0),
  ConditionStatus NVARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT N'Hazır',
  MissingNotes NVARCHAR(1500) NULL,
  Notes NVARCHAR(1500) NULL,
  RentalOrderItemId BIGINT NULL REFERENCES dbo.RentalOrderItems(RentalOrderItemId),
  UpdatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME()
 );
END
GO
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE name='UX_RentalInventory_ProductSerial' AND object_id=OBJECT_ID('dbo.RentalInventory'))
  CREATE UNIQUE INDEX UX_RentalInventory_ProductSerial ON dbo.RentalInventory(RentalProductId,SerialNo) WHERE SerialNo IS NOT NULL;
GO
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE name='IX_RentalInventory_Warehouse_Status' AND object_id=OBJECT_ID('dbo.RentalInventory'))
  CREATE INDEX IX_RentalInventory_Warehouse_Status ON dbo.RentalInventory(RentalWarehouseId,ConditionStatus,RentalProductId);
GO
IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.RentalStageHistory','U') IS NULL
BEGIN
 CREATE TABLE dbo.RentalStageHistory(
  RentalStageHistoryId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
  RentalOrderId BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.RentalOrders(RentalOrderId),
  StageName NVARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
  EventAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL,
  TeamName NVARCHAR(200) NULL,
  RentalWarehouseId INT NULL REFERENCES dbo.RentalWarehouses(RentalWarehouseId),
  Notes NVARCHAR(1500) NULL,
  SerialSnapshot NVARCHAR(MAX) NULL,
  CreatedByUserId INT NULL REFERENCES dbo.ErpUsers(UserId),
  CreatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME()
 );
END
GO
IF COL_LENGTH('dbo.RentalStageHistory','SerialSnapshot') IS NULL
  ALTER TABLE dbo.RentalStageHistory ADD SerialSnapshot NVARCHAR(MAX) NULL;
GO
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE name='IX_RentalStageHistory_Order' AND object_id=OBJECT_ID('dbo.RentalStageHistory'))
  CREATE INDEX IX_RentalStageHistory_Order ON dbo.RentalStageHistory(RentalOrderId,EventAt,RentalStageHistoryId);
GO
-- Eski tek ürünlü kiralamaları ilk kalem olarak koru (çift kayıt açma).
INSERT INTO dbo.RentalOrderItems(RentalOrderId,RentalProductId,Quantity,SerialNumbers)
SELECT r.RentalOrderId,r.RentalProductId,r.Quantity,r.SerialNumbers
FROM dbo.RentalOrders r
WHERE r.RentalProductId IS NOT NULL
  AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.RentalOrderItems i WHERE i.RentalOrderId=r.RentalOrderId);
GO
-- Eski irtibatı da kayıpsız yeni ilişki tablosuna taşı.
INSERT INTO dbo.RentalOrderContacts(RentalOrderId,ContactType,FullName,Phone)
SELECT r.RentalOrderId,N'Saha Operasyon',r.ContactName,r.ContactPhone
FROM dbo.RentalOrders r
WHERE NULLIF(LTRIM(RTRIM(r.ContactName)),N'') IS NOT NULL
AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM dbo.RentalOrderContacts c WHERE c.RentalOrderId=r.RentalOrderId);
GO



=================================== DOSYA: DengeERP.Api/Database/ZZ_V113_TrainingDevelopment.sql ==========

-- DengeERP V1.13 | Egitim, yetkinlik ve saha gelisim yonetimi.
-- Yalnizca yeni tablolar / tekrar calistirilabilir tohum veriler. Mevcut kayitlari SILMEZ.
IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.TrainingCourses',N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
  CREATE TABLE dbo.TrainingCourses (
    CourseId INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
    CourseCode NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    Title NVARCHAR(220) NOT NULL,
    Category NVARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    UnitCode NVARCHAR(100) NULL,
    Reason NVARCHAR(1200) NULL,
    TargetBehavior NVARCHAR(1200) NULL,
    SuccessMetric NVARCHAR(800) NULL,
    DurationHours DECIMAL(5,2) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_TrainingCourse_Duration DEFAULT (2),
    Mandatory BIT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_TrainingCourse_Mandatory DEFAULT (0),
    IsActive BIT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_TrainingCourse_Active DEFAULT (1),
    CreatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_TrainingCourse_Created DEFAULT SYSDATETIME()
  );
END
GO
IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.TrainingCriteria',N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
  CREATE TABLE dbo.TrainingCriteria (
    CriterionId INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
    CourseId INT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.TrainingCourses(CourseId),
    CriterionText NVARCHAR(450) NOT NULL,
    IsCritical BIT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_TrainingCriteria_Critical DEFAULT (0),
    SortOrder INT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_TrainingCriteria_Sort DEFAULT(0)
  );
  CREATE INDEX IX_TrainingCriteria_Course ON dbo.TrainingCriteria(CourseId,SortOrder);
END
GO
IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.TrainingPlans',N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
  CREATE TABLE dbo.TrainingPlans (
    TrainingPlanId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
    PersonnelId BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.Personnel(PersonnelId),
    CourseId INT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.TrainingCourses(CourseId),
    PlanYear INT NOT NULL CHECK(PlanYear BETWEEN 2020 AND 2100),
    DueDate DATE NOT NULL,
    Status NVARCHAR(30) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_TrainingPlans_Status DEFAULT N'Planlandı',
    PersonalGoal NVARCHAR(900) NULL,
    CompletedAt DATETIME2 NULL,
    CreatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_TrainingPlans_Created DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
    CONSTRAINT UQ_TrainingPlans_Year UNIQUE(PersonnelId,CourseId,PlanYear)
  );
  CREATE INDEX IX_TrainingPlans_Year ON dbo.TrainingPlans(PlanYear,DueDate,Status);
END
GO
IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.TrainingSessions',N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
  CREATE TABLE dbo.TrainingSessions (
    TrainingSessionId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
    CourseId INT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.TrainingCourses(CourseId),
    SessionDate DATE NOT NULL,
    TrainerName NVARCHAR(180) NOT NULL,
    DurationHours DECIMAL(5,2) NOT NULL CHECK(DurationHours>0 AND DurationHours<=24),
    Location NVARCHAR(250) NULL,
    Notes NVARCHAR(1200) NULL,
    CreatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_TrainingSessions_Created DEFAULT SYSDATETIME()
  );
END
GO
IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.TrainingAttendance',N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
  CREATE TABLE dbo.TrainingAttendance (
    TrainingAttendanceId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
    TrainingSessionId BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.TrainingSessions(TrainingSessionId),
    PersonnelId BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.Personnel(PersonnelId),
    Attended BIT NOT NULL,
    ScoreBefore INT NULL CHECK(ScoreBefore BETWEEN 0 AND 100),
    ScoreAfter INT NULL CHECK(ScoreAfter BETWEEN 0 AND 100),
    Notes NVARCHAR(900) NULL,
    CreatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_TrainingAttendance_Created DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
    CONSTRAINT UQ_TrainingAttendance_Person UNIQUE(TrainingSessionId,PersonnelId)
  );
END
GO
IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.CompetencyCatalog',N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
  CREATE TABLE dbo.CompetencyCatalog (
    CompetencyId INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
    Code NVARCHAR(60) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    Name NVARCHAR(220) NOT NULL,
    UnitCode NVARCHAR(100) NULL,
    SuggestedTargetLevel INT NOT NULL DEFAULT(3) CHECK(SuggestedTargetLevel BETWEEN 1 AND 4),
    IsCritical BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT(0)
  );
END
GO
IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.PersonnelCompetencies',N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
  CREATE TABLE dbo.PersonnelCompetencies (
    PersonnelCompetencyId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
    PersonnelId BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.Personnel(PersonnelId),
    CompetencyId INT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.CompetencyCatalog(CompetencyId),
    CurrentLevel INT NOT NULL CHECK(CurrentLevel BETWEEN 1 AND 4),
    TargetLevel INT NOT NULL CHECK(TargetLevel BETWEEN 1 AND 4),
    AssessedAt DATE NOT NULL,
    AssessedBy NVARCHAR(180) NOT NULL,
    Evidence NVARCHAR(1200) NULL,
    NextReviewDate DATE NULL,
    UpdatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
    CONSTRAINT UQ_PersonnelCompetencies UNIQUE(PersonnelId,CompetencyId)
  );
END
GO
IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.TrainingObservations',N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
  CREATE TABLE dbo.TrainingObservations (
    ObservationId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
    PersonnelId BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.Personnel(PersonnelId),
    CourseId INT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.TrainingCourses(CourseId),
    WorkOrderId BIGINT NULL REFERENCES dbo.WorkOrders(WorkOrderId),
    ObservedOn DATE NOT NULL,
    ObserverName NVARCHAR(180) NOT NULL,
    PhaseDays INT NOT NULL CHECK(PhaseDays IN(0,30,60,90)),
    Score DECIMAL(5,2) NOT NULL,
    CriticalFailure BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT(0),
    Notes NVARCHAR(1600) NULL,
    ActionPlan NVARCHAR(1600) NULL,
    NextReviewDate DATE NULL,
    CreatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME()
  );
  CREATE INDEX IX_TrainingObservations_Person ON dbo.TrainingObservations(PersonnelId,ObservedOn DESC);
END
GO
IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.TrainingObservationRatings',N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
  CREATE TABLE dbo.TrainingObservationRatings (
    ObservationId BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.TrainingObservations(ObservationId),
    CriterionId INT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.TrainingCriteria(CriterionId),
    Rating INT NOT NULL CHECK(Rating IN (-1,0,1,2)),
    PRIMARY KEY (ObservationId,CriterionId)
  );
END
GO
-- Ornek egitim katalogu: plan atamasi otomatik yapilmaz, yonetici secer.
INSERT INTO dbo.TrainingCourses(CourseCode,Title,Category,UnitCode,Reason,TargetBehavior,SuccessMetric,DurationHours,Mandatory)
SELECT s.Code,s.Title,s.Category,s.UnitCode,s.Reason,s.Behavior,s.Metric,s.Hours,s.Mandatory
FROM (VALUES
(N'ISG-SAH-01',N'Sahada İSG ve Güvenli Çalışma',N'İSG',CAST(NULL AS NVARCHAR(100)),N'Koruyucu ekipman ve güvenli çalışma adımlarındaki farklı uygulamaları azaltmak',N'Sahaya çıkmadan riskleri, KKD ve güvenli kaldırma uygulamalarını kontrol etmek',N'Saha gözleminde kritik İSG uygunsuzluğunun sıfırlanması',CAST(3 AS DECIMAL(5,2)),CAST(1 AS BIT)),

(N'KMD-BAK-01',N'Kapı Dedektörü Test ve Kalibrasyon',N'Teknik',N'Güvenlik Sistemleri',N'Kapı tipi metal dedektörlerinin kontrol ve testlerini standartlaştırmak',N'Doğru test, hassasiyet doğrulama ve kayıt oluşturmak',N'90. gün uygulamalı gözlemde en az %85 uygunluk',CAST(4 AS DECIMAL(5,2)),CAST(0 AS BIT)),

(N'MOB-01',N'Mobilite ve Araç Takip Saha Kontrolleri',N'Teknik',N'Mobilite',N'Saha kurulum ve test kalitesini yükseltmek',N'Kurulum, bağlantı, fonksiyon testi ve kayıtları standartlaştırmak',N'90. gün gözleminde %85 üzeri uygunluk',CAST(4 AS DECIMAL(5,2)),CAST(0 AS BIT))
) AS s(Code,Title,Category,UnitCode,Reason,Behavior,Metric,Hours,Mandatory)
WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.TrainingCourses x WHERE x.CourseCode=s.Code);
GO

(N'POC-01',N'Çözüm adımlarını ve sonucu kaydetti',0,3),
(N'MOB-01',N'Montaj ve kablo güvenliğini kontrol etti',1,1),
(N'MOB-01',N'Cihazın bağlantı ve seri bilgisini doğruladı',0,2),
(N'MOB-01',N'Konum/telemetri testini yaptı',0,3),
(N'MOB-01',N'Kurulum sonucunu ve saha notunu kaydetti',0,4)
) v(Code,Text,Critical,SortOrder)
JOIN dbo.TrainingCourses c ON c.CourseCode=v.Code
WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.TrainingCriteria tc WHERE tc.CourseId=c.CourseId AND tc.SortOrder=v.SortOrder);
GO
INSERT INTO dbo.CompetencyCatalog(Code,Name,UnitCode,SuggestedTargetLevel,IsCritical)
SELECT v.Code,v.Name,v.UnitCode,v.Target,v.Critical
FROM (VALUES
(N'ISG',N'İSG ve sahada güvenli çalışma',CAST(NULL AS NVARCHAR(100)),4,CAST(1 AS BIT)),
(N'RAPOR',N'Servis raporu ve kayıt disiplini',CAST(NULL AS NVARCHAR(100)),4,CAST(0 AS BIT)),
(N'ILETISIM',N'Müşteri iletişimi',CAST(NULL AS NVARCHAR(100)),3,CAST(0 AS BIT)),
(N'XR',N'X-Ray arıza teşhisi ve bakımı',N'Güvenlik Sistemleri',3,CAST(1 AS BIT)),
(N'KAPI',N'Kapı dedektörü test ve kalibrasyonu',N'Güvenlik Sistemleri',3,CAST(0 AS BIT)),
(N'RADYO',N'Telsiz ve röle arıza teşhisi',N'Haberleşme',3,CAST(0 AS BIT)),
(N'POC',N'POC uzaktan destek',N'POC',3,CAST(0 AS BIT)),
(N'MOB',N'Araç takip / mobilite kurulumu',N'Mobilite',3,CAST(0 AS BIT))
) v(Code,Name,UnitCode,Target,Critical)
WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.CompetencyCatalog x WHERE x.Code=v.Code);
GO

-- V1.14 - Canli saha zaman oturumlari. Tekrar calistirilabilir, onceki sure kayıtlarini etkilemez.
IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.WorkOrderLiveSessions',N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.WorkOrderLiveSessions
    (
        SessionId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT PK_WorkOrderLiveSessions PRIMARY KEY,
        WorkOrderId BIGINT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT FK_LiveSessions_WorkOrders REFERENCES dbo.WorkOrders(WorkOrderId),
        TechnicianUserId INT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT FK_LiveSessions_Users REFERENCES dbo.ErpUsers(UserId),
        TechnicianName NVARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
        ActivityType NVARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
        StartedAt DATETIME2(0) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_LiveSessions_StartedAt DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
        EndedAt DATETIME2(0) NULL,
        StopReason NVARCHAR(30) NULL,
        LocationNote NVARCHAR(250) NULL,
        WorkNote NVARCHAR(1000) NULL,
        ResultTimeLogId BIGINT NULL,
        CancelledByUserId INT NULL,
        CancelReason NVARCHAR(500) NULL,
        CONSTRAINT CK_LiveSessions_Activity CHECK (ActivityType IN (
            N'Saha Çalışması', N'Yol / Ulaşım', N'Bekleme',
            N'Uzaktan Destek', N'Atölye', N'Test / Kontrol', N'Mola / Ara')),
        CONSTRAINT CK_LiveSessions_ValidEnd CHECK (EndedAt IS NULL OR EndedAt>=StartedAt)
    );
END;
GO
IF NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE object_id=OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.WorkOrderLiveSessions') AND name=N'UX_LiveSessions_OneActivePerUser')
    CREATE UNIQUE INDEX UX_LiveSessions_OneActivePerUser
        ON dbo.WorkOrderLiveSessions(TechnicianUserId)
        WHERE EndedAt IS NULL;
GO
IF NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE object_id=OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.WorkOrderLiveSessions') AND name=N'IX_LiveSessions_WorkOrder')
    CREATE INDEX IX_LiveSessions_WorkOrder
        ON dbo.WorkOrderLiveSessions(WorkOrderId,StartedAt DESC)
        INCLUDE (EndedAt,TechnicianUserId,ActivityType);
GO



=================================== DOSYA: DengeERP.Api/Database/ZZ_V120_Operations.sql ==========

-- V1.20: idempotent additive SQL, no existing workorder modifications.
IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.OpsSlaTargets','U') IS NULL
 CREATE TABLE dbo.OpsSlaTargets(Priority NVARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,ResolutionHours INT NOT NULL CHECK(ResolutionHours BETWEEN 1 AND 8760));
GO
MERGE dbo.OpsSlaTargets AS t USING(VALUES(N'Kritik',12),(N'Yüksek',24),(N'Normal',72),(N'Düşük',120))s(Priority,ResolutionHours)
 ON t.Priority=s.Priority WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT(Priority,ResolutionHours) VALUES(s.Priority,s.ResolutionHours);
GO
IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.OpsHolds','U') IS NULL
 CREATE TABLE dbo.OpsHolds(HoldId BIGINT IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,WorkOrderId BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.WorkOrders(WorkOrderId),Reason NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,Notes NVARCHAR(1000) NULL,PreviousStatus NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,CreatedBy INT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.ErpUsers(UserId),CreatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),ReleasedAt DATETIME2 NULL);
GO
IF NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE object_id=OBJECT_ID('dbo.OpsHolds') AND name='UX_OpsHolds_Open')
 CREATE UNIQUE INDEX UX_OpsHolds_Open ON dbo.OpsHolds(WorkOrderId) WHERE ReleasedAt IS NULL;
GO
IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.OpsAlertAcks','U') IS NULL
 CREATE TABLE dbo.OpsAlertAcks(AlertKey NVARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,UserId INT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.ErpUsers(UserId),AckAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),CONSTRAINT PK_OpsAlertAcks PRIMARY KEY(AlertKey,UserId));
GO
IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.ServicePartCatalog','U') IS NULL
 CREATE TABLE dbo.ServicePartCatalog(PartId INT IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,Code NVARCHAR(80) NOT NULL UNIQUE,Name NVARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,Unit NVARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT N'Adet',MinimumQty DECIMAL(18,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK(MinimumQty>=0),UnitCost DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK(UnitCost>=0));
GO
IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.ServiceStockBalances','U') IS NULL
 CREATE TABLE dbo.ServiceStockBalances(PartId INT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.ServicePartCatalog(PartId),WarehouseId INT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.RentalWarehouses(RentalWarehouseId),Quantity DECIMAL(18,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK(Quantity>=0),CONSTRAINT PK_ServiceStockBalances PRIMARY KEY(PartId,WarehouseId));
GO
IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.ServicePartMovements','U') IS NULL
 CREATE TABLE dbo.ServicePartMovements(MovementId BIGINT IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,PartId INT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.ServicePartCatalog(PartId),WarehouseId INT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.RentalWarehouses(RentalWarehouseId),WorkOrderId BIGINT NULL REFERENCES dbo.WorkOrders(WorkOrderId),Kind NVARCHAR(10) NOT NULL CHECK(Kind IN(N'Giriş',N'Çıkış')),Quantity DECIMAL(18,3) NOT NULL CHECK(Quantity>0),UnitCost DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL,CreatedBy INT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.ErpUsers(UserId),CreatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME());
GO
IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.OpsLaborRates','U') IS NULL
 CREATE TABLE dbo.OpsLaborRates(UnitCode NVARCHAR(100) PRIMARY KEY,HourlyCost DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK(HourlyCost>=0));
GO
MERGE dbo.OpsLaborRates AS t USING(VALUES(N'Güvenlik Sistemleri'),(N'Haberleşme'),(N'POC'),(N'Mobilite'),(N'Araç Takip'))s(UnitCode)
 ON t.UnitCode=s.UnitCode WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT(UnitCode,HourlyCost) VALUES(s.UnitCode,0);
GO
IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.OpsWorkFinancials','U') IS NULL
 CREATE TABLE dbo.OpsWorkFinancials(WorkOrderId BIGINT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES dbo.WorkOrders(WorkOrderId),Revenue DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK(Revenue>=0),OtherCost DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK(OtherCost>=0),Notes NVARCHAR(1000) NULL,UpdatedBy INT NOT NULL REFERENCES dbo.ErpUsers(UserId),UpdatedAt DATETIME2 NOT NULL DEFAULT SYSDATETIME());
GO

=================================== DOSYA: DengeERP.Api/OpsSuite.cs ==========

using Microsoft.Data.SqlClient;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Text;

// V1.20 Operations: additional API endpoints, existing V1.14 API untouched.
internal static class OpsSuite
{
  private static bool Open(string s)=>s is not ("Kapatıldı" or "Tamamlandı" or "İptal");
  private static bool Maint(WorkOrderRow w)=>w.WorkOrderNo.StartsWith("PB-")||w.WorkType=="Planlı Bakım";
  private static object P(string? s)=>string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s)?DBNull.Value:s.Trim();
  private static async Task<SqlConnection> Db(string c){var db=new SqlConnection(c);await db.OpenAsync();return db;}
  private sealed record Alert(string Key,string Severity,string Message,long? WorkOrderId);
  private static async Task<Dictionary<string,int>> Targets(string conn)
  {
    var t=new Dictionary<string,int>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    await using var db=await Db(conn);using var cmd=new SqlCommand("SELECT Priority,ResolutionHours FROM dbo.OpsSlaTargets",db);
    await using var r=await cmd.ExecuteReaderAsync();while(await r.ReadAsync())t[r.GetString(0)]=r.GetInt32(1);
    return t;
  }
  private static async Task<Dictionary<long,string>> Holds(string conn)
  {
    var h=new Dictionary<long,string>();await using var db=await Db(conn);
    using var cmd=new SqlCommand("SELECT WorkOrderId,Reason FROM dbo.OpsHolds WHERE ReleasedAt IS NULL",db);
    await using var r=await cmd.ExecuteReaderAsync();while(await r.ReadAsync())h[r.GetInt64(0)]=r.GetString(1);return h;
  }
  private static List<Alert> Alerts(List<WorkOrderRow> rows,Dictionary<string,int> sla,Dictionary<long,string> holds)
  {
    var a=new List<Alert>();var now=DateTime.Now;
    foreach(var w in rows.Where(x=>Open(x.Status)))
    {
      var t=w.CreatedAt.AddHours(sla.GetValueOrDefault(w.Priority,72));
      if(t<now)a.Add(new($"sla:{w.WorkOrderId}:{now:yyyyMMdd}","Kritik",$"Geciken: {w.WorkOrderNo} • {w.CustomerName}",w.WorkOrderId));
      if(w.PlanDate==DateOnly.FromDateTime(now.AddDays(1)))a.Add(new($"plan:{w.WorkOrderId}:{now:yyyyMMdd}","Bilgi",$"Yarın: {w.WorkOrderNo} • {w.CustomerName}",w.WorkOrderId));
      if(holds.TryGetValue(w.WorkOrderId,out var reason))a.Add(new($"hold:{w.WorkOrderId}:{now:yyyyMMdd}","Uyarı",$"Beklemede: {w.WorkOrderNo} • {reason}",w.WorkOrderId));
    }
    return a;
  }
  private static async Task<List<Alert>> AuxiliaryAlerts(string conn,SessionUser u,Func<SessionUser,string,bool> can,
    Func<SessionUser,string,bool> unitAllowed)
  {
    var list=new List<Alert>();await using var db=await Db(conn);
    if(can(u,"warehouse.view"))
    {
      using var cmd=new SqlCommand(@"SELECT p.PartId,p.Code,p.MinimumQty,COALESCE(SUM(b.Quantity),0)
FROM dbo.ServicePartCatalog p LEFT JOIN dbo.ServiceStockBalances b ON b.PartId=p.PartId
WHERE p.MinimumQty>0 GROUP BY p.PartId,p.Code,p.MinimumQty HAVING COALESCE(SUM(b.Quantity),0)<p.MinimumQty",db);
      await using var r=await cmd.ExecuteReaderAsync();while(await r.ReadAsync())list.Add(new(
        $"stock:{r.GetInt32(0)}:{DateTime.Today:yyyyMMdd}","Uyarı",$"Düşük stok: {r.GetString(1)} ({r.GetValue(3)} / {r.GetValue(2)})",null));
    }
    if(can(u,"training.view"))
    {
      using var cmd=new SqlCommand(@"SELECT TOP(40) p.TrainingPlanId,c.Title,s.FullName,p.DueDate,COALESCE(s.UnitCode,N'')
FROM dbo.TrainingPlans p JOIN dbo.TrainingCourses c ON p.CourseId=c.CourseId
JOIN dbo.Personnel s ON p.PersonnelId=s.PersonnelId
WHERE p.Status<>N'Tamamlandı' AND p.DueDate<=DATEADD(DAY,7,CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE)) ORDER BY p.DueDate",db);
      await using var r=await cmd.ExecuteReaderAsync();while(await r.ReadAsync())
      {
        string unit=r.GetString(4);
        if(!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(unit)&&!unitAllowed(u,unit))continue;
        list.Add(new($"train:{r.GetInt64(0)}:{DateTime.Today:yyyyMMdd}","Bilgi",
          $"Eğitim tarihi: {r.GetString(2)} / {r.GetString(1)} ({r.GetDateTime(3):dd.MM.yyyy})",null));
      }
    }
    return list.Take(80).ToList();
  }
  private static string Csv(params object?[] fields)=>string.Join(";",fields.Select(x=>{
    var v=Convert.ToString(x,CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)??"";
    if(v.Length>0&&"=+-@\t\r".Contains(v[0]))v="'"+v;
    return "\""+v.Replace("\"","\"\"")+"\"";
  }));
  public static void Map(WebApplication app,string conn,Func<HttpContext,SessionUser?> current,
    Func<SessionUser,string,bool> can,Func<SessionUser,string,bool> unitAllowed,
    Func<Task<List<WorkOrderRow>>> loadOrders, Func<List<WorkOrderRow>,SessionUser,List<WorkOrderRow>> filterOrders,
    Func<Task<List<TimeLogRow>>> loadLogs)
  {
    app.MapGet("/api/ops/overview",async(HttpContext ctx,int? days)=>
    {
      var u=current(ctx);if(u is null)return Results.Unauthorized();
      if(!can(u,"dashboard.view"))return Results.Forbid();
      var n=Math.Clamp(days??30,1,366);var rows=filterOrders(await loadOrders(),u);
      var targets=await Targets(conn);var holds=await Holds(conn);
      var logs=await loadLogs();var minutes=logs.GroupBy(x=>x.WorkOrderId).ToDictionary(x=>x.Key,x=>x.Sum(y=>y.Minutes));
      var eligible=rows.Where(w=>w.CreatedAt>=DateTime.Now.AddDays(-n)||Open(w.Status)).ToList();
      var data=eligible.Select(w=>new{ id=w.WorkOrderId,no=w.WorkOrderNo,unit=w.UnitCode,type=w.WorkType,
        customer=w.CustomerName,location=w.Location,city=w.SiteCity,subject=w.Subject,priority=w.Priority,status=w.Status,
        createdAt=w.CreatedAt,closedAt=w.ClosedAt,planDate=w.PlanDate,assigned=w.AssignedStaff,chief=w.ChiefName,
        isMaintenance=Maint(w),overdue=Open(w.Status)&&DateTime.Now>w.CreatedAt.AddHours(targets.GetValueOrDefault(w.Priority,72)),
        holdReason=holds.GetValueOrDefault(w.WorkOrderId),recordedMinutes=minutes.GetValueOrDefault(w.WorkOrderId,0)}).ToList();
      var person=eligible.SelectMany(w=>(w.AssignedStaff??"").Split(',',StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries|StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries)
        .Select(p=>new{name=p,order=w})).GroupBy(x=>x.name,StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
        .Select(g=>new{name=g.Key,total=g.Count(),open=g.Count(x=>Open(x.order.Status)),closed=g.Count(x=>!Open(x.order.Status))})
        .OrderByDescending(x=>x.open).ToList();
      var repeat=eligible.Where(w=>!Maint(w)).SelectMany(w=>(w.SerialNumbers??"").Split(',',StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries|StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries)
        .Select(serial=>new{serial,order=w})).GroupBy(x=>x.serial,StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
        .Where(g=>g.Count()>1).Select(g=>new{serialNo=g.Key,count=g.Count(),orders=g.Select(x=>x.order.WorkOrderNo).Take(8)})
        .OrderByDescending(x=>x.count).Take(25).ToList();
      var alerts=Alerts(rows,targets,holds);
      alerts.AddRange(await AuxiliaryAlerts(conn,u,can,unitAllowed));
      var seen=await Acks(conn,u.UserId);
      return Results.Ok(new{
        generatedAt=DateTime.Now,
        counters=new{open=data.Count(x=>Open(x.status)),overdue=data.Count(x=>x.overdue),service=data.Count(x=>!x.isMaintenance),
          maintenance=data.Count(x=>x.isMaintenance),today=data.Count(x=>x.planDate==DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today)),
          tomorrow=data.Count(x=>x.planDate==DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today.AddDays(1))),
          hold=data.Count(x=>x.holdReason is not null),closed=data.Count(x=>!Open(x.status))},
        orders=data.OrderByDescending(x=>x.overdue).ThenByDescending(x=>x.createdAt).Take(1500),
        personnel=person,repeats=repeat,alerts=alerts.Where(x=>!seen.Contains(x.Key)).Take(100),
        sla=targets.Select(x=>new{priority=x.Key,hours=x.Value}).OrderBy(x=>x.hours)
      });
    });
    app.MapPost("/api/ops/alerts/ack",async(HttpContext ctx,AckRequest req)=>
    {
      var u=current(ctx);if(u is null)return Results.Unauthorized();if(!can(u,"dashboard.view"))return Results.Forbid();
      var rows=filterOrders(await loadOrders(),u);var alarms=Alerts(rows,await Targets(conn),await Holds(conn));
      alarms.AddRange(await AuxiliaryAlerts(conn,u,can,unitAllowed));
      if(!alarms.Any(x=>x.Key==req.Key))return Results.NotFound();
      await using var db=await Db(conn);using var cmd=new SqlCommand(@"IF NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM dbo.OpsAlertAcks WHERE AlertKey=@Key AND UserId=@User)
INSERT INTO dbo.OpsAlertAcks(AlertKey,UserId) VALUES(@Key,@User)",db);
      cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Key",req.Key);cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@User",u.UserId);await cmd.ExecuteNonQueryAsync();
      return Results.Ok(new{message="Uyarı görüldü olarak işaretlendi."});
    });
    app.MapPut("/api/ops/sla/{priority}",async(HttpContext ctx,string priority,SlaRequest req)=>
    {
      var u=current(ctx);if(u is null)return Results.Unauthorized();if(!can(u,"operations.manage"))return Results.Forbid();
      if(req.Hours<1||req.Hours>8760)return Results.BadRequest(new{message="Geçerli bir saat hedefi girin."});
      await using var db=await Db(conn);using var cmd=new SqlCommand("UPDATE dbo.OpsSlaTargets SET ResolutionHours=@Hours WHERE Priority=@Priority",db);
      cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Hours",req.Hours);cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Priority",priority);
      return await cmd.ExecuteNonQueryAsync()==0?Results.NotFound():Results.Ok(new{message="SLA güncellendi."});
    });
    app.MapPost("/api/ops/workorders/{id:long}/hold",async(HttpContext ctx,long id,HoldRequest req)=>
    {
      var u=current(ctx);if(u is null)return Results.Unauthorized();
      if(!can(u,"operations.manage")&&!can(u,"workorders.edit"))return Results.Forbid();
      if(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(req.Reason)||req.Reason.Length>100||req.Notes?.Length>1000)return Results.BadRequest(new{message="Neden/not kontrol edin."});
      var w=filterOrders(await loadOrders(),u).FirstOrDefault(w=>w.WorkOrderId==id);
      if(w is null)return Results.NotFound();if(!Open(w.Status))return Results.BadRequest(new{message="Kapalı iş bekletilemez."});
      await using var db=await Db(conn);await using var tx=(SqlTransaction)await db.BeginTransactionAsync();
      using(var cmd=new SqlCommand(@"INSERT dbo.OpsHolds(WorkOrderId,Reason,Notes,PreviousStatus,CreatedBy)
VALUES(@Id,@Reason,@Notes,@Previous,@User);
UPDATE dbo.WorkOrders SET Status=N'Beklemede',UpdatedAt=SYSDATETIME() WHERE WorkOrderId=@Id;",db,tx)){
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Id",id);cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Reason",req.Reason);
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Notes",P(req.Notes));cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Previous",w.Status);
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@User",u.UserId);await cmd.ExecuteNonQueryAsync();}
      await tx.CommitAsync();return Results.Ok(new{message="Bekleme kaydedildi."});
    });
    app.MapPost("/api/ops/workorders/{id:long}/release",async(HttpContext ctx,long id)=>
    {
      var u=current(ctx);if(u is null)return Results.Unauthorized();
      if(!can(u,"operations.manage")&&!can(u,"workorders.edit"))return Results.Forbid();
      if(!filterOrders(await loadOrders(),u).Any(w=>w.WorkOrderId==id))return Results.NotFound();
      await using var db=await Db(conn);await using var tx=(SqlTransaction)await db.BeginTransactionAsync();
      using var cmd=new SqlCommand(@"UPDATE dbo.OpsHolds SET ReleasedAt=SYSDATETIME() OUTPUT INSERTED.PreviousStatus
WHERE WorkOrderId=@Id AND ReleasedAt IS NULL",db,tx);cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Id",id);
      var previous=(string?)await cmd.ExecuteScalarAsync();
      if(previous is null){await tx.RollbackAsync();return Results.BadRequest(new{message="Aktif bekleme kaydı yok."});}
      using var up=new SqlCommand("UPDATE dbo.WorkOrders SET Status=@Previous,UpdatedAt=SYSDATETIME() WHERE WorkOrderId=@Id AND Status=N'Beklemede'",db,tx);
      up.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Previous",previous);up.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Id",id);await up.ExecuteNonQueryAsync();
      await tx.CommitAsync();return Results.Ok(new{message="Bekleme kaldırıldı."});
    });
    app.MapGet("/api/ops/stock",async(HttpContext ctx)=>
    {
      var u=current(ctx);if(u is null)return Results.Unauthorized();if(!can(u,"warehouse.view"))return Results.Forbid();
      var stocks=new List<object>();var wh=new List<object>();
      await using var db=await Db(conn);
      using(var cmd=new SqlCommand("SELECT RentalWarehouseId,Name FROM dbo.RentalWarehouses WHERE IsActive=1",db)){
        await using var r=await cmd.ExecuteReaderAsync();while(await r.ReadAsync())wh.Add(new{id=r.GetInt32(0),name=r.GetString(1)});}
      using(var cmd=new SqlCommand(@"SELECT p.PartId,p.Code,p.Name,p.Unit,p.MinimumQty,p.UnitCost,
 b.WarehouseId,COALESCE(b.Quantity,0),COALESCE(w.Name,N'Kayıt bekliyor')
FROM dbo.ServicePartCatalog p LEFT JOIN dbo.ServiceStockBalances b ON b.PartId=p.PartId
LEFT JOIN dbo.RentalWarehouses w ON w.RentalWarehouseId=b.WarehouseId ORDER BY p.Name",db)){
        await using var r=await cmd.ExecuteReaderAsync();while(await r.ReadAsync())stocks.Add(new{
          id=r.GetInt32(0),code=r.GetString(1),name=r.GetString(2),unit=r.GetString(3),minimum=r.GetDecimal(4),
          cost=can(u,"finance.prices.view")?(decimal?)r.GetDecimal(5):null,
          warehouseId=r.IsDBNull(6)?(int?)null:r.GetInt32(6),quantity=r.GetDecimal(7),warehouse=r.GetString(8)});}
      return Results.Ok(new{warehouses=wh,stocks});
    });
    app.MapPost("/api/ops/stock/part",async(HttpContext ctx,PartRequest req)=>
    {
      var u=current(ctx);if(u is null)return Results.Unauthorized();if(!can(u,"warehouse.manage"))return Results.Forbid();
      if(req.Code.Length is <1 or >80||req.Name.Length is <1 or >200||req.Minimum<0||req.Cost<0)return Results.BadRequest();
      if(req.Cost>0&&!can(u,"finance.costs.manage"))return Results.Forbid();
      await using var db=await Db(conn);using var cmd=new SqlCommand(@"INSERT dbo.ServicePartCatalog(Code,Name,Unit,MinimumQty,UnitCost)
VALUES(@Code,@Name,@Unit,@Minimum,@Cost)",db);
      cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Code",req.Code.Trim());cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Name",req.Name.Trim());
      cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Unit","Adet");cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Minimum",req.Minimum);cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Cost",req.Cost);
      await cmd.ExecuteNonQueryAsync();return Results.Ok(new{message="Parça kaydedildi."});
    });
    app.MapPost("/api/ops/stock/movement",async(HttpContext ctx,MovementRequest req)=>
    {
      var u=current(ctx);if(u is null)return Results.Unauthorized();if(!can(u,"warehouse.manage"))return Results.Forbid();
      if(req.Quantity<=0||req.Quantity>100000||req.Kind is not("Giriş" or "Çıkış"))return Results.BadRequest();
      if(req.Kind=="Çıkış"&&!req.WorkOrderId.HasValue)return Results.BadRequest(new{message="Çıkış için iş emri zorunlu."});
      if(req.WorkOrderId.HasValue&&!filterOrders(await loadOrders(),u).Any(w=>w.WorkOrderId==req.WorkOrderId.Value))return Results.Forbid();
      await using var db=await Db(conn);await using var tx=(SqlTransaction)await db.BeginTransactionAsync(System.Data.IsolationLevel.Serializable);
      using var price=new SqlCommand(@"SELECT p.UnitCost FROM dbo.ServicePartCatalog p
JOIN dbo.RentalWarehouses w ON w.RentalWarehouseId=@Wh AND w.IsActive=1 WHERE p.PartId=@Part",db,tx);
      price.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Wh",req.WarehouseId);price.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Part",req.PartId);
      var cost=await price.ExecuteScalarAsync();if(cost is null){await tx.RollbackAsync();return Results.BadRequest(new{message="Parça / depo bulunamadı."});}
      using var bal=new SqlCommand("SELECT Quantity FROM dbo.ServiceStockBalances WITH(UPDLOCK,HOLDLOCK) WHERE PartId=@Part AND WarehouseId=@Wh",db,tx);
      bal.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Part",req.PartId);bal.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Wh",req.WarehouseId);
      decimal before=Convert.ToDecimal(await bal.ExecuteScalarAsync()??0);decimal after=before+(req.Kind=="Giriş"?req.Quantity:-req.Quantity);
      if(after<0){await tx.RollbackAsync();return Results.BadRequest(new{message="Stok yetersiz."});}
      using var update=new SqlCommand(@"IF EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM dbo.ServiceStockBalances WHERE PartId=@Part AND WarehouseId=@Wh)
UPDATE dbo.ServiceStockBalances SET Quantity=@Qty WHERE PartId=@Part AND WarehouseId=@Wh
ELSE INSERT dbo.ServiceStockBalances(PartId,WarehouseId,Quantity) VALUES(@Part,@Wh,@Qty);
INSERT dbo.ServicePartMovements(PartId,WarehouseId,WorkOrderId,Kind,Quantity,UnitCost,CreatedBy)
VALUES(@Part,@Wh,@Work,@Kind,@MoveQty,@Cost,@User);",db,tx);
      update.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Part",req.PartId);update.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Wh",req.WarehouseId);
      update.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Qty",after);update.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Work",req.WorkOrderId.HasValue?(object)req.WorkOrderId.Value:DBNull.Value);
      update.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Kind",req.Kind);update.Parameters.AddWithValue("@MoveQty",req.Quantity);
      update.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Cost",Convert.ToDecimal(cost));update.Parameters.AddWithValue("@User",u.UserId);
      await update.ExecuteNonQueryAsync();await tx.CommitAsync();return Results.Ok(new{message="Stok hareketi kaydedildi.",remaining=after});
    });
    app.MapGet("/api/ops/finance",async(HttpContext ctx)=>
    {
      var u=current(ctx);if(u is null)return Results.Unauthorized();
      if(!can(u,"finance.prices.view")||!can(u,"reports.view"))return Results.Forbid();
      var rows=filterOrders(await loadOrders(),u);var logs=await loadLogs();var result=new List<object>();
      var rates=new Dictionary<string,decimal>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
      var money=new Dictionary<long,(decimal Revenue,decimal Other,decimal Material)>();
      await using var db=await Db(conn);
      using(var cmd=new SqlCommand("SELECT UnitCode,HourlyCost FROM dbo.OpsLaborRates",db)){
        await using var r=await cmd.ExecuteReaderAsync();while(await r.ReadAsync())rates[r.GetString(0)]=r.GetDecimal(1);}
      using(var cmd=new SqlCommand(@"SELECT w.WorkOrderId,COALESCE(f.Revenue,0),COALESCE(f.OtherCost,0),
 COALESCE((SELECT SUM(CASE WHEN m.Kind=N'Çıkış' THEN m.Quantity*m.UnitCost ELSE -m.Quantity*m.UnitCost END)
 FROM dbo.ServicePartMovements m WHERE m.WorkOrderId=w.WorkOrderId),0)
FROM dbo.WorkOrders w LEFT JOIN dbo.OpsWorkFinancials f ON f.WorkOrderId=w.WorkOrderId",db)){
        await using var r=await cmd.ExecuteReaderAsync();while(await r.ReadAsync())money[r.GetInt64(0)]=(r.GetDecimal(1),r.GetDecimal(2),Convert.ToDecimal(r.GetValue(3)));}
      foreach(var w in rows){var m=money.GetValueOrDefault(w.WorkOrderId);
        var hours=logs.Where(x=>x.WorkOrderId==w.WorkOrderId).Sum(x=>x.Minutes)/60m;
        var labor=decimal.Round(hours*rates.GetValueOrDefault(w.UnitCode),2);
        var total=m.Other+m.Material+labor;
        result.Add(new{id=w.WorkOrderId,no=w.WorkOrderNo,unit=w.UnitCode,customer=w.CustomerName,revenue=m.Revenue,
          other=m.Other,material=m.Material,labor,total,margin=m.Revenue-total,hours});}
      return Results.Ok(new{orders=result,rates=rates.Where(x=>unitAllowed(u,x.Key)).Select(x=>new{unit=x.Key,cost=x.Value})});
    });
    app.MapPut("/api/ops/finance/rate",async(HttpContext ctx,RateRequest req)=>
    {
      var u=current(ctx);if(u is null)return Results.Unauthorized();
      if(!can(u,"finance.prices.view")||!can(u,"finance.costs.manage")||!unitAllowed(u,req.Unit))return Results.Forbid();
      if(req.Cost<0||req.Cost>1000000)return Results.BadRequest();
      await using var db=await Db(conn);using var cmd=new SqlCommand("UPDATE dbo.OpsLaborRates SET HourlyCost=@Cost WHERE UnitCode=@Unit",db);
      cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Cost",req.Cost);cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Unit",req.Unit);
      return await cmd.ExecuteNonQueryAsync()==0?Results.NotFound():Results.Ok(new{message="Tarife kaydedildi."});
    });
    app.MapPut("/api/ops/finance/{id:long}",async(HttpContext ctx,long id,FinanceRequest req)=>
    {
      var u=current(ctx);if(u is null)return Results.Unauthorized();
      if(!can(u,"finance.prices.view")||!can(u,"finance.costs.manage"))return Results.Forbid();
      if(!filterOrders(await loadOrders(),u).Any(w=>w.WorkOrderId==id))return Results.Forbid();
      if(req.Revenue<0||req.Other<0||req.Revenue>1000000000||req.Other>1000000000)return Results.BadRequest();
      await using var db=await Db(conn);using var cmd=new SqlCommand(@"IF EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM dbo.OpsWorkFinancials WHERE WorkOrderId=@Id)
UPDATE dbo.OpsWorkFinancials SET Revenue=@Revenue,OtherCost=@Other,Notes=@Notes,UpdatedBy=@User,UpdatedAt=SYSDATETIME() WHERE WorkOrderId=@Id
ELSE INSERT dbo.OpsWorkFinancials(WorkOrderId,Revenue,OtherCost,Notes,UpdatedBy) VALUES(@Id,@Revenue,@Other,@Notes,@User)",db);
      cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Id",id);cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Revenue",req.Revenue);
      cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Other",req.Other);cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Notes",P(req.Notes));cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@User",u.UserId);
      await cmd.ExecuteNonQueryAsync();return Results.Ok(new{message="Maliyet kaydı tamam."});
    });
    app.MapGet("/api/ops/export.csv",async(HttpContext ctx)=>
    {
      var u=current(ctx);if(u is null)return Results.Unauthorized();if(!can(u,"reports.export"))return Results.Forbid();
      var lines=new List<string>{Csv("İş Emri","Birim","Müşteri","Tür","Durum","Oluşturma","Plan","Teknisyen")};
      foreach(var w in filterOrders(await loadOrders(),u))lines.Add(Csv(w.WorkOrderNo,w.UnitCode,w.CustomerName,w.WorkType,w.Status,w.CreatedAt,w.PlanDate,w.AssignedStaff));
      return Results.File(Encoding.UTF8.GetPreamble().Concat(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(string.Join("\r\n",lines))).ToArray(),"text/csv; charset=utf-8","DengeERP_Operasyon.csv");
    });
  }
  private static async Task<HashSet<string>> Acks(string conn,int userId)
  {
    var result=new HashSet<string>();await using var db=await Db(conn);
    using var cmd=new SqlCommand("SELECT AlertKey FROM dbo.OpsAlertAcks WHERE UserId=@Id",db);cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Id",userId);
    await using var r=await cmd.ExecuteReaderAsync();while(await r.ReadAsync())result.Add(r.GetString(0));return result;
  }
  internal sealed record AckRequest(string Key);
  internal sealed record SlaRequest(int Hours);
  internal sealed record HoldRequest(string Reason,string? Notes);
  internal sealed record PartRequest(string Code,string Name,decimal Minimum,decimal Cost);
  internal sealed record MovementRequest(int PartId,int WarehouseId,long? WorkOrderId,string Kind,decimal Quantity);
  internal sealed record RateRequest(string Unit,decimal Cost);
  internal sealed record FinanceRequest(decimal Revenue,decimal Other,string? Notes);
}



=================================== DOSYA: DengeERP.Api/Program.cs ==========

using Microsoft.Data.SqlClient;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO.Compression;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Mail;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);
builder.WebHost.UseUrls(builder.Configuration["Urls"] ?? "http://0.0.0.0:5050");
var app = builder.Build();

app.UseDefaultFiles();
app.UseStaticFiles();

var connectionString = builder.Configuration.GetConnectionString("DengeERP")
    ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("ConnectionStrings:DengeERP ayarlanmamis. /etc/dengeerp/dengeerp.env dosyasini kontrol edin.");

var sessions = new ConcurrentDictionary<string, SessionUser>();
var databasePath = Path.Combine(app.Environment.ContentRootPath, "Database");

// Linux kurulumu: varsayilan olarak veri tabaninda HICBIR sema/seed degisikligi yapma.
// Yalnizca IT onayi ve test DB yedegi sonrasinda DengeERP__RunDatabaseSetupOnStartup=true.
if (builder.Configuration.GetValue<bool>("DengeERP:RunDatabaseSetupOnStartup"))
{
    try
    {
        foreach (var sqlFile in Directory.GetFiles(databasePath, "*.sql").OrderBy(Path.GetFileName))
            await ApplySchemaAsync(connectionString, sqlFile);

        await SeedUsersAsync(connectionString, builder.Configuration["DengeERP:InitialAdminPassword"]);
        await EnsureMaintenancePlansAndWorkOrdersAsync(connectionString);
    }
    catch (Exception ex)
    {
        app.Logger.LogError(ex, "DengeERP veritabani ilklendirme basarisiz; hizmet baslatilmadi.");
        throw;
    }
}
else
{
    app.Logger.LogInformation("Veritabani sema guncellemesi KAPALI. Var olan test veritabani kullanilacak.");
}

app.MapGet("/api/health", async () =>
{
    try
    {
        await using var connection = new SqlConnection(connectionString);
        await connection.OpenAsync();
        return Results.Ok(new
        {
            application = "Denge ERP API",
            status = "Çalışıyor",
            database = "Bağlı",
            time = DateTime.Now
        });
    }
    catch (Exception ex)
    {
        return Results.Problem(title: "SQL bağlantısı başarısız", detail: ex.Message, statusCode: 503);
    }
});

app.MapPost("/api/auth/login", async (LoginRequest request) =>
{
    await using var connection = new SqlConnection(connectionString);
    await connection.OpenAsync();

    var command = new SqlCommand(@"
SELECT UserId,Username,PasswordHash,PasswordSalt,FullName,RoleName,AllowedUnits,Permissions,IsActive,MustChangePassword
FROM dbo.ErpUsers
WHERE Username=@Username", connection);
    command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Username", request.Username.Trim());

    await using var reader = await command.ExecuteReaderAsync();
    if (!await reader.ReadAsync()) return Results.Unauthorized();

    var userId = reader.GetInt32(0);
    var username = reader.GetString(1);
    var hash = reader.GetString(2);
    var salt = reader.GetString(3);
    var fullName = reader.GetString(4);
    var roleName = reader.GetString(5);
    var units = SplitPipe(reader.GetString(6));
    var permissions = SplitPipe(reader.GetString(7));
    var isActive = reader.GetBoolean(8);
    var mustChange = reader.GetBoolean(9);

    if (!isActive || !VerifyPassword(request.Password, salt, hash)) return Results.Unauthorized();

    var token = Convert.ToHexString(RandomNumberGenerator.GetBytes(32));
    var session = new SessionUser(userId, username, fullName, roleName, units, permissions, mustChange);
    sessions[token] = session;

    return Results.Ok(new { token, user = session });
});

app.MapGet("/api/auth/me", (HttpContext ctx) =>
{
    var user = GetCurrentUser(ctx, sessions);
    return user is null ? Results.Unauthorized() : Results.Ok(user);
});

app.MapPost("/api/auth/logout", (HttpContext ctx) =>
{
    var token = ReadToken(ctx);
    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(token)) sessions.TryRemove(token, out _);
    return Results.Ok(new { message = "Oturum kapatıldı." });
});

app.MapPost("/api/auth/change-password", async (HttpContext ctx, ChangePasswordRequest request) =>
{
    var user = GetCurrentUser(ctx, sessions);
    if (user is null) return Results.Unauthorized();
    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.NewPassword) || request.NewPassword.Length < 8)
        return Results.BadRequest(new { message = "Yeni şifre en az 8 karakter olmalı." });

    var (salt, hash) = HashPassword(request.NewPassword);
    await using var connection = new SqlConnection(connectionString);
    await connection.OpenAsync();
    var command = new SqlCommand(@"
UPDATE dbo.ErpUsers
SET PasswordHash=@Hash,PasswordSalt=@Salt,MustChangePassword=0,UpdatedAt=SYSDATETIME()
WHERE UserId=@Id", connection);
    command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Hash", hash);
    command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Salt", salt);
    command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Id", user.UserId);
    await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync();

    var refreshed = user with { MustChangePassword = false };
    var token = ReadToken(ctx);
    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(token)) sessions[token] = refreshed;
    return Results.Ok(new { message = "Şifre güncellendi.", user = refreshed });
});
